Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The universe operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 705. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 164 with 3 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on universe structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 705. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 164. QED.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the recursion lemma condition on universe structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 319. The convergence follows from the recursion lemma estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 790. QED.
